- **workflow-release**: Releases a goal once it has a pull request. With auto_merge on, `merge` merges it and publishes the release (no draft), through a release PR on a protected base. Use after workflow-delivery opens a pull request, or when asked to release or not release a goal. ([Charlie-org146/agent-workflow-template](https://github.com/Charlie-org146/agent-workflow-template/tree/HEAD/skills/workflow-release))
